By aligning features through semantic and distributional constraints rather than explicit weather modeling, DCDA generalizes effectively to unseen weather types and severities without requiring paired data or weather labels. We further introduce a structured open-weather benchmark with held-out type-severity combinations and extensive experiments verify DCDA's advantages.

</details>


### [70] [Understanding Geometric Representations in Self-Supervised Vision Transformers via Subspace Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01987)
*Weichen Zhou,Yawen Zou,Chunzhi Gu,Ran Dong,Haoran Xie,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出受控子空间干预框架，用SVD分解线性探针权重以定位ViT中显式几何信息所在的低秩子空间，揭示不同自监督目标的几何编码差异、几何表示可压缩性及层间任务亲和规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性探针常用于评估视觉Transformer的几何表征能力，但通常将特征视为黑箱，难以解释几何信息在特征空间中的具体拓扑结构、子空间分布以及不同预训练目标造成的编码机制差异。

Method: 作者在收敛后的线性探针权重上进行奇异值分解（SVD），分离包含显式几何信号的低秩子空间，并通过受控子空间干预分析不同模型、不同层以及不同空间上下文条件下几何信息的可提取性和压缩性。

Result: 研究发现：DINOv2更倾向于将空间几何特征对齐到便于线性提取的方向；MAE则更分散地编码几何信号，需要更大的空间上下文；显式几何表示高度可压缩；几何精度通常在中间层达到峰值，最终层更偏向语义抽象。

Conclusion: 该工作将ViT内部几何编码机制与下游密集预测性能联系起来，为特征层选择、低秩约束预测头和轻量级解码器设计提供了实证依据与分析框架。

Abstract: We introduce a controlled subspace intervention framework to investigate how self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) encode dense geometric information. While linear probing is widely used to assess geometric representations, it treats features as a black box, failing to disentangle the underlying topology. To address this issue, we decompose the weights of converged linear probes to isolate the low-rank subspaces containing explicit geometric signals using Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Our perspective yields three key insights: (1) Pre-training objectives determine how features are encoded. DINOv2 aligns spatial features for efficient linear extraction, while Masked Autoencoders (MAE) tend to disperse these signals, requiring a broader spatial context. (2) Explicit geometric representations are highly compressible, suggesting dense predictive heads could potentially be constrained to low-rank subspaces with minimal performance loss. (3) The layer-wise task affinity suggests that geometric precision peaks at intermediate layers before yielding to semantic abstraction in the final layers. By connecting internal encoding mechanics with downstream performance, these findings provide a basis for effective feature selection and lightweight decoder design. The source code is available at https://github.com/Zhou-Weichen/Geosubprobe.

</details>


### [71] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的机制可解释方法，用于定位并干预 CLIP/ViT 中导致 Typographic Attack 的词汇偏置电路，从而提升 LVLM 在含无关文字图像上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 作为现代大视觉语言模型的核心视觉编码器，容易被图像中的无关文本干扰，使视觉表征偏向文字含义而非真实视觉语义；这一 Typographic Attack 在自动驾驶等安全关键场景中存在风险，但其机制来源尚未充分解释。

Method: 作者提出训练无关的机制可解释框架：通过采样式隐藏状态解释、对注意力头进行语义/词汇关注的定量归因、概率分析与 circuit mining，识别 ViT 中过度编码词汇信息的组件；随后对这些已定位电路进行直接干预，如选择性调整注意力权重。

Result: 所提出的电路级干预在对象分类中的 Typographic Attack 防御效果显著，优于监督式和其他训练无关防御方法；将该干预应用于多种先进 LVLM 的视觉编码器后，在 RIO-Bench 的 Typographic Attack 干扰下显著提升 VQA 准确率。

Conclusion: 论文表明 CLIP/ViT 中存在可定位的词汇偏置机制电路，是 Typographic Attack 的关键来源；通过无需额外训练的可解释干预可以有效且可泛化地提升 LVLM 对图像中文字干扰的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [72] [Training-free Controllable Human Motion Generation under Heterogeneous Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01990)
*Xiaofei Hui,Bo Yan,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIC：一种无需训练的可控运动生成框架，将基于扩散的运动生成表述为随机控制问题，可统一处理连续可微目标约束与离散/稀疏/黑盒准则约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 training-free 可控运动生成方法通常依赖连续目标函数和可微损失，但现实需求常表现为基于准则的反馈，可能是不连续、稀疏甚至黑盒的，因此需要一种无需约束特定训练且不依赖可微性的统一方法。

Method: 作者将扩散式运动生成建模为随机控制问题，推导逐步控制律，使 criterion-based 约束无需可微损失即可介入生成过程；同时将 objective-based 约束视为特例纳入同一机制，并设计面向控制的约束协调机制，自适应平衡和调和多个运动约束。

Result: 在多种约束设置下的实验表明，MIC 能有效处理不同类型的运动约束，并在灵活性和约束满足能力上优于或补足现有 training-free 方法。

Conclusion: MIC 为可控运动生成提供了一个统一、无需训练、可处理黑盒/离散准则反馈的框架，扩展了扩散运动生成在真实复杂约束场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Training-free controllable motion generation has attracted growing interest for enabling flexible constraint enforcement without constraint-specific training. However, existing training-free methods require constraints to be continuous objective-based with differentiable losses, while many real-world requirements are criterion-based and provide only discontinuous, sparse, or even black-box feedback. In this paper, we propose Motion-Inference-as-Control (MIC), the first training-free motion generation framework that handles both continuous objective-based and criterion-based motion constraints under a shared mechanism. The key idea is to cast diffusion-based motion generation as a stochastic control problem. This perspective not only provides principled and practically effective step-wise control laws that support criterion-based constraints without requiring differentiability and naturally accommodate objective-based constraints as a special case, but also motivates a control-oriented constraint coordination mechanism that adaptively balances and reconciles motion constraints during generation. Experiments across diverse constraint settings demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [73] [Mirror Illusion Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02015)
*Xiaopei Zhu,Zeyuan Li,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoMIA 是一种自动化镜面幻觉艺术设计流水线，可从正面与镜中两张目标图像反向设计可打印的 3D 物体，并联合优化几何形状与纹理颜色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有镜面幻觉艺术设计方法通常依赖拓扑或阴影策略，人工参与多、主要只优化形状，且生成结果容易出现表面噪声、几何不连续、不平滑或内部断裂等问题，难以高效地产生可制造的高质量作品。

Method: 论文将任务建模为从两张 2D 目标图像到带几何与纹理的 3D 可打印物体的逆向设计问题，提出 AutoMIA 流水线进行形状与颜色联合优化。为稳定优化并抑制伪影，引入四个机制：投影对齐组件选择（PAC）降低表面噪声；位置加权自适应抑制（PWA）减少背景噪声；内部体素保留（IVP）避免内部断裂；形状-颜色解耦优化（SCD）平衡几何与颜色优化。

Result: AutoMIA 能在数字环境和物理打印场景中生成多样且平滑的镜面幻觉艺术作品；在单张 RTX 3090 上平均设计时间约 76 秒、显存占用约 2.6 GB，显示出较高效率与实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了反向图形学与计算设计在镜面幻觉艺术中的应用，通过自动化、联合几何纹理优化及多种稳定化机制，显著降低人工成本并提升生成物体的平滑性、完整性和可制造性。

Abstract: Mirror Illusion Art is a novel reflection-conditioned 3D illusion where one object yields two target appearances (front and mirror). The task is formulated as inverse design from two target 2D images (front and mirror) to a printable 3D object with geometry and texture. Prior topology-driven and shadow-based approaches demand substantial manual effort, optimize shape only, and often yield non-smooth or incomplete geometry. To address these challenges, we propose AutoMIA, an automated Mirror Illusion Art design pipeline that jointly optimizes shape and color. To stabilize optimization and suppress artifacts, four mechanisms are introduced: (1) projection-alignment component (PAC) selection to reduce surface noise, (2) position-weighted adaptive (PWA) suppression for background noise, (3) internal voxel preservation (IVP) to prevent internal fractures, and (4) shape-color decoupled (SCD) optimization that balance shape and color optimization. AutoMIA generate diverse smooth Mirror Illusion artworks successfully both in the digital and physical world, with only around 76s design time and 2.6 GB memory on average using a single RTX 3090, advancing inverse graphics and computational design. Our code is available at https://github.com/zxp555/AutoMIA.

</details>


### [74] [UnderOneFacade: Worldwide Facade Semantic Segmentation Benchmark Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02018)
*Yi Wang,Fan Wang,Prabin Gyawali,Ziyang Xu,Anna Klimkowska,Yixiong Jing,Wanru Yang,Filip Biljecki,Christoph Holst,Benjamin Busam,Brian Sheil,Olaf Wysocki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UnderOneFacade，一个超大规模、跨国家与跨大陆的 3D 建筑立面分割基准，用于评估模型的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 立面解析数据集存在地域覆盖窄、语义标注不统一、几何精度不足等问题，难以支撑厘米级精度、语义一致且具有地理可迁移性的数字孪生建模。

Method: 构建包含 27 亿个标注点的厘米级精度点云数据集，采用层次化、统一化且基于建筑学语义的标签体系；并系统评估代表性的点云、图结构和 Transformer 类 3D 分割模型。

Result: 实验表明，当前方法在细粒度建筑元素识别上表现不足，并且在跨地理域测试时性能显著下降；最佳模型在细粒度 LoFG3 基准上仅达到最高 33 IoU。

Conclusion: UnderOneFacade 通过大规模、高精度、标准化语义的 3D 立面数据，为开发更鲁棒、可迁移的 3D 分割模型提供了严格基准。

Abstract: Globally consistent semantic digital twins require centimeter-accurate and geographically transferable 3D facade segmentation. However, progress in facade parsing is limited by the lack of large-scale, standardized benchmarks for evaluating cross-domain generalization. Existing datasets are geographically narrow, semantically inconsistent, or insufficiently precise. We introduce UnderOneFacade, the largest cross-country and cross-continent 3D facade benchmark to date, comprising centimeter-accurate point clouds with hierarchical, harmonized, and architecturally grounded semantic labels totaling 2.7 billion annotated points. Through a systematic evaluation of representative point-, graph- and transformer-based architectures, we show that current methods struggle to recognize fine-grained architectural elements and degrade significantly across geographic domains, with the best models achieving only up to 33 IoU on the fine-grained LoFG3 benchmark. By combining geometric precision with standardized semantics at unprecedented scale, UnderOneFacade establishes a rigorous benchmark for developing robust and transferable 3D segmentation models. The dataset, evaluation scripts, and pretrained models will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [75] [Spatio-Temporal and Clinical Conditioning for Fine-Grained Radiology Report Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02024)
*P. Sloan,E. Simpson,M. Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR3 是一种用于胸部 X 光自动报告生成的多模态时空注意力检索框架，通过结合解剖区域、临床指征与纵向变化，提高检索式报告生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射影像需求增长与放射科医生短缺导致报告负担加重；现有检索式方法较为僵硬，缺乏明确的解剖定位，也未充分利用既往检查中的疾病进展信息和临床上下文。

Method: 方法使用目标检测器识别具有解剖意义的胸片区域，并在当前临床指征和既往—当前检查变化的条件下，检索语义相关的报告句子；整体框架通过区域级解剖信息、时间变化和临床文本进行多模态对齐与注意力检索。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上的实验表明，STAR3 在检索指标、自然语言生成指标和临床相关指标上均优于现有检索式基线方法。

Conclusion: 将解剖定位、纵向时序变化和临床上下文共同纳入检索过程，有助于生成更符合临床报告实践的放射报告，并推动自动化放射报告生成的发展。

Abstract: Radiology is vital to modern healthcare, but rising imaging demand and persistent workforce shortages strain reporting capacity and clinical workflows. Automated radiology report generation has the potential to support radiologists and help alleviate this burden; however, existing retrieval-based methods remain rigid, lack explicit anatomical grounding, and do not account for longitudinal disease progression or available clinical context. In this work, we introduce STAR3, a multimodal, spatio-temporal, attentive retrieval framework for radiology report generation that aligns region-level anatomical information with clinical indications and longitudinal changes across chest X-ray studies. Our framework employs an object detector to identify anatomically meaningful regions and retrieves semantically relevant report sentences conditioned on both current clinical context and changes observed between prior and current examinations. This design enables anatomically and temporally grounded report generation that better reflects clinical reporting practice. Experiments on the MIMIC-CXR dataset demonstrate that STAR3 outperforms current retrieval-based approaches on retrieval, NLP and clinical metrics, highlighting the value of conditioning retrieval anatomically, temporally and clinically for advancing automated radiology report generation.

</details>


### [76] [Evaluating Vision-Language Models as a Zero-Shot Learning Alternative to You Only Look Once and Optical Character Recognition for Nigerian License Plate Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02025)
*Ismail Ismail Tijjani,Ahmad Abubakar Mustapaha,Sunusi Ibrahim Muhammad,Muhammad Bashir Aliyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估视觉语言模型在尼日利亚车牌识别中的零样本能力，发现 Gemini 与 Qwen 在字符错误率和鲁棒性上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统车牌识别通常依赖 YOLO 目标检测与 OCR 的多阶段流程，存在资源消耗高、非结构化真实场景表现不稳定、需要大量标注数据等问题。研究希望探索 VLM 是否能作为统一的零样本方案，降低系统复杂度并提升复杂场景适应性。

Method: 研究构建了包含 88 张尼日利亚真实复杂场景车牌图像的数据集，并评估五个视觉语言模型：Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp、Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct、GPT-4o、Claude 4 Sonnet 和 Llama 3.2 Vision 90b。以字符错误率（CER）作为主要评价指标，对模型识别准确性与鲁棒性进行比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，Gemini 与 Qwen 在挑战性图像场景下明显优于其他模型，在准确率和鲁棒性方面表现更强。研究还指出，不同 VLM 的实际表现与模型提供方的能力宣称之间可能存在差距。

Conclusion: VLM 在车牌识别任务中展现出替代传统 YOLO+OCR 流水线的潜力，尤其适用于标注数据有限和真实环境复杂的场景。但摘要中数据规模较小，结论仍需在更大、更系统的数据集上验证。

Abstract: License Plate Recognition (LPR) systems are critical tools in traffic monitoring, security enforcement, and urban mobility management. Traditional LPR systems often rely on a multi-stage pipeline involving object detection using You Only Look Once (YOLO) and Optical Character Recognition (OCR), which suffer from limitations such as high resource demands, poor performance in unstructured environments, and the need for large annotated datasets. This study explores the potential of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified, zeroshot learning solution for Nigerian license plate recognition. Using a curated dataset of 88 challenging real-world images collected in Nigeria, we evaluate five selected VLMs: Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp (Google DeepMind), Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct (Alibaba), GPT-4o (OpenAI), Claude 4 Sonnet (Anthropic), and Llama 3.2 Vision 90b (Meta). Results based on Character Error Rate (CER) reveal that Gemini and Qwen significantly outperform other models in both accuracy and robustness, on the challenging image scenarios. This work highlights the practical advantages of VLMs over YOLO+OCR, questions the claims by model providers, and compares the performances of the VLMs.

</details>


### [77] [ComplexMimic: Human-Scene Interaction Imitation in Complex 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02034)
*Lu Pan,Hongwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ComplexMimic，用于复杂环境中的物理式人-场景交互模仿学习，通过双流专家与难度感知蒸馏，在交互成功率与动作自然性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理式 HSI 模仿方法多面向简化场景，难以处理复杂环境；复杂场景中，准确完成交互与保持自然、物理合理动作之间存在内在权衡。

Method: 提出 ComplexMimic 框架解释并重建含缺陷的 MoCap 数据；设计 Dual Flow Strategy，分别学习用于精确运动跟踪的模仿专家和用于复杂场景中碰撞感知适应的交互专家；进一步提出 difficulty-aware distillation，根据失败统计与学习进展自适应加权监督，优先学习困难但可学习的轨迹。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComplexMimic 通过专家互补与难度感知知识蒸馏提升了复杂环境下 HSI 模仿的鲁棒性和表现，为更真实场景中的具身智能交互提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Physics-based Human-Scene Interaction (HSI) imitation learning is crucial for embodied intelligence as it bridges the gap between kinematic 3D motions and real-world dynamics. However, most existing methods focus on simplified scene settings, leaving complex environments largely unexplored, which limits their applicability in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we focus on HSI mimicry in complex environments. Under this complex setting, we observe an inherent trade-off between successfully performing interaction and maintaining natural, physically plausible motions. To address this challenge, we propose ComplexMimic, a framework that reconstructs diverse HSI by interpreting imperfect MoCap data. First, we introduce a Dual Flow Strategy, which learns two complementary experts: an imitation expert for accurate motion tracking and an interaction expert for collision-aware adaptation in complex scenes. Second, naive multi-expert distillation, which treats all experts equally, often under-samples challenging behaviors, limiting effective learning. To mitigate this issue, we propose a difficulty-aware distillation strategy that adaptively weights supervision and prioritizes hard-yet-learnable trajectories guided by failure statistics and learning progress signals. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/LuPan23/ComplexMimic.

</details>


### [78] [Hierarchical Anti-Aesthetics: Protecting Facial Privacy against Customized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02038)
*Songping Wang,Yueming Lyu,Shiqi Liu,Chen Zhao,Ziyuan Chen,Ning Li,Jing Dong,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出层次化反美学（HAA）框架，通过降低定制化扩散模型生成图像的整体美感与局部人脸质量，减少面部身份泄露，提升隐私保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化/定制化扩散模型促进了个性化视觉内容生成，但也可能被滥用来复现或泄露个人面部身份。作者观察到图像美学质量与人类感知质量高度相关，因此希望从“降低生成美学质量”的新角度削弱恶意定制模型的身份生成能力。

Method: 方法包含两个分支：全局反美学分支通过构造全局反美学奖励机制及损失函数，降低整体图像美感和生成质量；局部反美学分支通过局部反美学奖励机制和损失函数，引导对抗扰动集中作用于人脸区域，从而破坏面部身份特征。两者结合形成从全局到局部的层次化反美学退化策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，HAA在身份移除方面优于现有方法，能够更有效地降低恶意定制模型生成图像中的面部身份保真度。

Conclusion: HAA提供了一种基于美学退化的人脸隐私保护思路，通过同时削弱整体生成质量和局部人脸身份表达，有效减少定制化扩散模型中的面部身份泄露风险。

Abstract: The rise of customized diffusion models has fueled a boom in personalized visual content creation, but it also introduces serious risks of malicious misuse, thereby posing threats to personal privacy. Image aesthetics are strongly correlated with human perception of image quality. Motivated by this observation, we address facial privacy protection from a novel aesthetic perspective by degrading the generation quality of maliciously customized models, thus reducing facial identity leakage. Specifically, we propose a Hierarchical Anti-Aesthetics (HAA) framework that exploits aesthetic cues at multiple perceptual levels. HAA consists of two key branches: (1) Global Anti-Aesthetics, which degrades overall aesthetics and generation quality by constructing a global anti-aesthetic reward mechanism and a corresponding loss; and (2) Local Anti-Aesthetics, which disrupts facial identity by using a local anti-aesthetic reward mechanism and loss to guide adversarial perturbations toward facial regions. By integrating both branches, HAA achieves anti-aesthetic degradation from a global to a local level during customized generation. Extensive experiments show that HAA outperforms existing methods in identity removal, providing an effective tool for protecting facial privacy.

</details>


### [79] [PWM-ArtGen: Part World Model for Articulated Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02045)
*Wentao Zheng,Ancong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PWM-ArtGen，通过联合学习视觉动态与运动学参数的分布，从单张图像生成可关节运动的 3D 物体，并利用无标注图像对进行协同训练，显著提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成关节式 3D 物体的核心难点在于准确预测底层运动学结构。现有方法要么直接从静态图像推断运动学参数，缺乏动态部件关系；要么先生成视觉动态再估计参数，容易产生两阶段误差累积。同时，现有带标注数据集规模和多样性不足，限制了真实复杂物体上的泛化。

Method: 作者将关节物体建模为动态系统，提出统一的 Part World Model：PWM-ArtGen。该模型联合建模视觉动态和运动学参数，并将动作扩散与图像扩散耦合，同时使用独立的扩散时间步以支持视觉分支协同训练。为利用无运动学标注数据，作者还构建了 19.7k 个照片级真实的部件级图像对数据集。

Result: 实验表明，PWM-ArtGen 在静止状态下显著优于现有基线方法，并在分布外物体上表现出较强的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一建模视觉动态与运动学结构，并引入无标注数据协同训练，有效缓解了单图关节式 3D 生成中的动态关系缺失、误差累积和数据不足问题，提升了真实场景泛化能力。

Abstract: The key challenge in articulated 3D object generation from a single image is accurately predicting the underlying kinematic structure. Existing methods either infer kinematic parameters directly from a static image that lacks dynamic part-level kinematic relationships, or estimate parameters from visual dynamics generated from a single image, which is prone to accumulated errors of two steps. Moreover, the limited scale and diversity of existing annotated datasets further hinder generalization to complex, real-world objects. To overcome these limitations, we propose to learn the joint distribution of visual dynamics and kinematic parameters. Recognizing that articulated objects can be formulated as dynamic systems, we propose a unified Part World Model called PWM-ArtGen. To leverage unannotated data, this model couples action diffusion and image diffusion with independent diffusion timesteps, which enables visual branch co-training. We further curate a photorealistic dataset of 19.7k part-level image pairs without kinematic annotations, to support co-training. Experiments demonstrate that PWM-ArtGen substantially outperforms existing baselines in the resting state and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to out-of-distribution objects.

</details>


### [80] [Embracing Intra-Class Heterogeneity for Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation: From Diversity to Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02051)
*Yuqi Liu,Yufei Chen,Wei Fu,Xiaodong Yue,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MPCL 半监督医学图像分割框架，通过多原型对比学习显式建模同一解剖结构内部由强度差异体现的异质性，在少量标注数据下提升分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SSMIS 方法往往将同一类别结构表示得过于统一，未充分利用医学图像中广泛存在的类内强度异质性；而标注数据稀缺进一步增加了学习复杂异质结构表示的难度，导致分割边界和区域预测不够精确。

Method: 方法包含三部分：1）强度对齐异质原型生成 IHPG，根据强度特征为同一结构生成多个原型以刻画类内异质性；2）原型空间优化 PSO，构建更具判别性和泛化性的原型空间；3）双分支知识对齐 DKA，将原型空间中的类内异质性知识迁移到分割网络，以提升最终预测质量。

Result: 在三个具有显著类内异质性的医学图像数据集上，MPCL 相比现有方法取得显著更优表现，尤其在极少标注数据设置下优势更明显。

Conclusion: MPCL 证明了在半监督医学图像分割中显式建模强度相关的类内异质性十分有效，多原型表示与知识对齐机制能够提升结构表征多样性和分割精度。

Abstract: Due to the scarcity of expert-annotated data, Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation (SSMIS) has emerged as a promising approach. Many anatomical structures in medical images exhibit significant intra-class heterogeneity, with different regions showing heterogeneous intensity patterns within the same structure. However, existing methods inadequately exploit this intensity-manifested intra-class heterogeneity, resulting in uniform structural representations and imprecise segmentation. Furthermore, the scarcity of labeled data makes it more difficult to effectively capture such complex heterogeneity. To address this, we propose Multiple Prototype Contrastive Learning (MPCL), an SSMIS framework that possesses better diversity and better precision. It consists of three novel designs: First, we provide structural representations with better diversity and propose Intensity-aligned Heterogeneous Prototype Generation (IHPG) that effectively models intra-class heterogeneity by generating multiple prototypes aligned with intensity characteristics. Second, we further enhance more diverse structural representations and build a solid foundation for more precise segmentation through Prototypical Space Optimization (PSO) that systematically optimizes a more discriminative and generalizable prototypical space. Finally, we achieve segmentation results with better precision through Dual-branch Knowledge Alignment (DKA) that efficiently promotes intra-class heterogeneity knowledge transfer from prototypical space to the segmentation network. Extensive experiments on three medical image datasets with significant intra-class heterogeneity demonstrate that MPCL significantly outperforms existing methods, especially under extremely limited labeled data.

</details>


### [81] [Comprehensive Robustness Analysis of LiDAR-based 3D Object Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02074)
*Adwait Chandorkar,Kai Krink,Yerdana Maulenbay,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向 LiDAR-only 3D 目标检测的整体性对抗鲁棒性评估框架，并发现近期先进模型在对抗攻击下仍然脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR-only 3D 目标检测研究主要关注基准数据集上的检测精度，而对抗鲁棒性验证不足；少量相关研究也多局限于旧模型。此外，现有评估框架过度依赖 mAP，忽略点云结构因素和预测行为因素，难以全面刻画模型在攻击下的失效模式。

Method: 作者提出一种整体评估框架，从两个结构因素——点云密度、点云定位，以及三个预测因素——误分类、定位误差、与自车距离，对模型鲁棒性进行分析。基于该框架，作者使用专门针对 LiDAR 模型设计的对抗攻击，对近期和传统的先进 3D 检测模型进行经验评估与对比分析。

Result: 研究发现，高容量的 voxel-based 检测器相比 pillar-based 检测器更容易受到结构化坐标扰动影响；non-anchor-based 检测器表现出较差的对抗鲁棒性。总体上，近期模型与旧模型一样容易受到对抗攻击。

Conclusion: 仅以检测精度提升作为 3D 目标检测模型评估标准是不充分的。未来基准测试应同时考察架构设计是否提升对抗鲁棒性，并需要重新思考训练策略与模型设计，以增强 LiDAR-only 3D 检测器在对抗场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in LiDAR-only 3D object detection have demonstrated improved detection accuracy over benchmark datasets. However, the adversarial robustness of these models remains untested. Very few adversarial robustness studies exist for LiDAR-only 3D object detection and unfortunately, even they are limited to legacy models. Moreover, there is a systemic gap in the existing evaluation frameworks that rely simply on mAP ignoring other structural and predictive factors. To fill this gap, we propose a holistic framework that evaluates adversarial robustness using two structural factors (point cloud density and point cloud localization) and three predictive factors (misclassification, localization error, distance from ego). Using this framework, we perform an empirical study and critical analysis on recent and legacy state-of-the-art models using adversarial attacks specifically designed for LiDAR-based models. Our key finding is that high-capacity, voxel-based detectors are more susceptible to structured coordinate perturbations than pillar-based detectors. Additionally, non-anchor-based detectors demonstrate poor adversarial robustness, which necessitates rethinking model training techniques. Overall, our results demonstrate that recent models are as vulnerable to adversarial attacks as their predecessors. Therefore, we argue that there is a need to improve the evaluation benchmarks for 3D object detection that not only reward architectural modifications for improving detection accuracy, but also evaluate whether the design choices improve adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [82] [HandsOnWorld: Unconstrained Egocentric Video Generation with Camera-Disentangled Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02075)
*Yushuo Chen,Xiaoyu Shi,Xiaoshi Wu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandsOnWorld提出一种从非受控单目第一视角视频中学习的手控自我中心视频生成框架，并构建大规模3D手部轨迹数据集EgoVid-Pro；通过Plücker Hand Map解耦相机运动与手部运动，在重建保真度、控制精度和跨场景泛化上优于既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手控视频生成依赖多视角或标记式动捕，数据采集受限；大规模第一视角视频缺少精细手指级3D标注，而精确手部数据集通常来自受控实验环境，导致模型难以覆盖多样日常场景并泛化到真实非受控场景。

Method: 作者先用单目重建在野外第一视角视频上直接生成3D手部标注，并设计以主体为中心的筛选流程，从动作语义、图像质量和3D几何一致性三个层面过滤，构建EgoVid-Pro数据集。随后提出Plücker Hand Map，将Plücker射线表示从相机射线扩展到手部表面，形成3D感知控制信号，以在表示层面解耦大幅自我运动带来的相机-手部耦合。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在重建保真度和手部控制准确性方面超过此前手控生成器，并能泛化到实验室数据集之外的日常开放场景。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于用可扩展的单目重建与质量过滤机制弥补真实第一视角视频中3D手部标注稀缺问题，并通过更合适的3D控制表示提升手控生成模型在复杂自我运动和真实场景下的可控性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We present HandsOnWorld, a framework for hand-controlled egocentric video generation that forgoes multi-view and marker-based motion capture, learning instead from unconstrained monocular video. Such generality is bottlenecked by the scarcity of scalable 3D hand annotations: large egocentric corpora lack finger-level labels, whereas precise hand datasets are confined to narrow, instrumented settings, limiting prior hand-controlled generators to restricted scene distributions. We instead annotate 3D hands directly on in-the-wild egocentric video through monocular reconstruction, introducing a protagonist-centered annotation pipeline that filters the reconstructions at the action-semantic, image-quality, and 3D-geometric levels to build EgoVid-Pro, a dataset of clean, protagonist-only hand trajectories spanning 103K clips and roughly 12M frames across diverse everyday scenes. To resolve the camera-hand entanglement induced by large ego-motion, we further propose the Plücker Hand Map, a 3D-aware control signal that extends Plücker-ray representations from camera rays to the hand surface, disentangling camera and hand motion at the representation level. Experiments show that \method surpasses prior hand-controlled generators in reconstruction fidelity and control accuracy, and generalizes to out-of-distribution everyday scenes beyond the laboratory datasets on which prior methods rely.

</details>


### [83] [DeepGaze3.5-VL: Modeling Scanpaths via Autoregressive Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02083)
*Susmit Agrawal,Matthias Bethge,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将人类视觉扫描路径预测重构为离散序列建模问题，通过把注视坐标与属性映射到文本词表，利用预训练视觉-语言模型实现灵活条件化，并在多个数据集上取得新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扫描路径模型依赖专门架构和手工先验，虽能建模注视序列，但结构偏置较强，难以灵活扩展到任务指令、观看者个体差异或逐注视属性等条件。

Method: 作者将坐标离散化为文本 token，把扫描路径作为自回归序列预测；基于视觉-语言模型的预训练表示，通过提示词注入全局条件，并将注视持续时间等逐注视属性与空间位置共同建模；利用自回归概率精确计算逐注视 log-likelihood，并与信息增益指标对应。

Result: 提出的 DeepGaze3.5-VL 在多个数据集上达到 SOTA，在 MIT1003 上取得 2.18 bits 信息增益，相比 DeepGaze III 提升 46%；即使基线使用相同高容量视觉编码器，优势仍然存在。

Conclusion: 将扫描路径预测视为通用离散序列生成任务，可显著提升性能和可扩展性，并可作为行为干预的计算工具，通过受控仿真恢复已知眼动现象。

Abstract: Understanding human visual attention on a scene over time has applications in domains such as interface design and inferring cognitive states. Modeling visual scanpaths has historically relied on specialized architectures with hand-crafted priors. While these architectures can model fixation sequences, their rigid structural biases restrict easy extendability and flexible conditioning. For instance, integrating task-specific instructions or adapting to distinct viewer identities requires custom, disjoint architectural additions. We frame scanpath prediction purely as a discrete sequence modeling task. By mapping coordinates into a text vocabulary, we leverage the pretrained representations of Vision-Language Models. This framing absorbs diverse factors of variation: simple prompting allows for global conditioning, such as providing viewer identities to capture personalized biases, or task-specific objectives like visual search. The framework can also integrate per-fixation attributes, such as individual fixation durations, alongside spatial locations. The autoregressive alignment enables the scalable, exact computation of per-fixation log-likelihoods, directly equivalent to the commonly used Information Gain (IG) metric. Our model, DeepGaze3.5-VL, establishes a new state-of-the-art across multiple datasets, achieving 2.18 bits of IG on MIT1003, a 46% improvement over DeepGaze III. This advantage persists even when baselines use identical high-capacity vision encoders. Beyond predictive performance, our generative framework serves as a powerful computational tool for direct behavioral interventions, allowing for controlled in-silico simulations that would be experimentally difficult or impossible to conduct in vivo. We demonstrate this ability by performing controlled interventions on the durations of pre-saccadic fixations, recovering known oculomotor phenomena purely from data.

</details>


### [84] [TCG-AR: Real-Time Multi-View Augmented Reality for Trading Card Game Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02090)
*Anthony Cioppa,Antoine Verdonck,Maxim Henry,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Raphaël La Rocca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TCG-AR：一种仅使用普通 RGB 摄像头、无需标记物或专用硬件的实时集换式卡牌增强现实直播管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卡牌直播多为平面俯拍，观赛体验有限；已有增强系统通常依赖带传感器的桌面或嵌入式芯片，成本高且不适合普通玩家和大规模活动。

Method: 系统通过 RGB 图像完成卡牌检测、方向估计与身份识别，并在多视角中将虚拟内容渲染到对应卡牌上；还可生成面向观众的广播式汇总视图并推流到 OBS 等软件。为减少人工标注，作者从卡牌参考图像自动生成带标注的合成训练数据，用于训练检测、方向和识别模型。

Result: 作者在新的人工标注真实图像数据集上评估多种模型，分析其识别性能与实时吞吐率，以验证实际使用可行性。

Conclusion: TCG-AR 证明了仅依赖消费级摄像头和普通硬件即可实现实时卡牌识别与 AR 直播增强；通过开源代码、模型和数据集，工作可作为实时卡牌识别与大众化 AR 直播的参考基础。

Abstract: Trading card games are increasingly played and broadcast online, yet live streams remain mostly limited to flat top-down footage of the playing area. Augmenting such streams with virtual models of the played cards would improve the viewing experience, but most existing systems rely on instrumented playing surfaces and embedded chips, which are costly and impractical for casual players and large-scale events. In this work, we present TCG-AR, a novel real-time pipeline that augments trading card games using ordinary RGB cameras alone, without any physical markers or specialized hardware. Our pipeline detects, orients, and identifies the cards on the board, renders virtual content onto each card across all views, and can additionally compose a broadcaststyle view that summarizes the game state for spectators, streaming the augmented feeds to standard broadcasting software such as OBS. To train the detection, orientation, and identification models without manual labeling, we introduce an automatic procedure that generates annotated synthetic training data from a reference set of card images. Then, we evaluate several trained models on a new manually annotated dataset with real images, analyzing performance and runtime throughput that determine real-world usability. Overall, by relying only on commodity cameras and hardware, and by open-sourcing all code, models, and datasets, this work aims to serve as a reference for real-time trading card recognition and to make real-time augmented-reality streaming accessible to the broader community of players and streamers.

</details>


### [85] [Multimodal Fusion for Fine-Grained Classification of Breast Fibroadenoma and Phyllodes Tumors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02091)
*Chuxi Nan,Di Wu,Hongming Guo,Ning Cao,Xiaohui Zhu,Zhaoting Shi,Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个用于乳腺纤维腺瘤（FA）与叶状肿瘤（PT）鉴别的病理确认多模态数据集 FAPT-M，并提出融合超声图像、临床属性和超声文本描述的多模态分类框架，在五折交叉验证中取得优于多类基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FA 与 PT 在 B 模式超声上表现高度相似，尤其良性和交界性 PT 容易被误判为 FA，影响术前决策；现有 CAD 方法多依赖单一影像模态，未充分利用临床信息与文本诊断描述的互补价值。

Method: 作者构建包含 910 名患者的病理确认多模态数据集，涵盖严格审核的超声图像、结构化临床属性和超声诊断描述；方法上采用 DenseNet 进行视觉编码、类 CLIP 的文本编码和轻量级临床编码，并引入临床条件自适应调制、跨模态 Transformer 融合和双路径表征学习以增强模态对齐与交互。

Result: 在患者级五折交叉验证下，模型达到 77.64% 准确率、73.38% F1-score 和 89.74% AUC，优于代表性的 CNN、Transformer 和视觉语言模型基线；消融实验和类别均衡评估验证了三模态融合及关键结构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为 FA-PT 精细分类提供了有效的临床引导多模态方案，同时建立了一个高质量的乳腺超声多模态分析基准，有助于改进术前鉴别诊断与临床决策支持。

Abstract: Breast fibroadenoma (FA) and phyllodes tumor (PT) are fibroepithelial breast lesions with highly overlapping appearances on B-mode ultrasound, making benign and borderline PT prone to being misclassified as FA and complicating preoperative decision-making. Existing computer-aided diagnosis methods commonly rely on single-modal imaging features and insufficiently exploit complementary clinical and textual information. To address this limitation, we construct the FAPT-M Dataset, a pathology-confirmed multimodal dataset comprising 910 patients with strictly reviewed ultrasound images, structured clinical attributes, and ultrasound diagnostic descriptions. Based on this dataset, we propose a clinically guided multimodal framework that integrates DenseNet-based visual encoding, CLIP-inspired text encoding, and lightweight clinical encoding, and further introduces clinical-conditioned adaptive modulation, cross-modal Transformer fusion, and dual-path representation learning to improve feature alignment and multimodal interaction. Under patient-level five-fold cross-validation, the proposed method achieves an accuracy of 77.64%, F1-score of 73.38%, and AUC of 89.74%, outperforming representative CNN-, Transformer-, and vision-language-based baselines. Ablation studies and class-balanced evaluations further confirm the contribution of three-modality fusion and the key architectural components. Overall, this work provides an effective multimodal approach for fine-grained FA-PT classification and establishes a high-quality benchmark for multimodal breast ultrasound analysis.

</details>


### [86] [LongEgoRefer: A Benchmark for Long-Form Egocentric Video Referring Expression Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02096)
*Shunya Kato,Taiki Miyanishi,Shuhei Kurita,Mahiro Ukai,Nakamasa Inoue,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LongEgoRefer，一个面向长时长第一视角视频的 Video REC 基准，用于评估模型在长视频中根据文本定位目标物体的时空范围；实验表明现有方法表现仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一视角 Video REC 基准多基于短视频片段，目标物体在帧中出现较密集，难以反映真实第一视角记录中长时长、未裁剪、目标稀疏、活动转换复杂等特点，因此需要更具挑战性的长视频时空定位 benchmark。

Method: 作者基于 Ego4D 长视频构建 LongEgoRefer，包含 1,498 条指代表达，平均视频时长约 45 分钟；任务要求模型依据自然语言查询同时判断事件发生时间以及目标物体在对应视频帧中的空间位置。作者评估了现有 Video REC 方法，以及结合视觉语言模型与 Grounded SAM2 的训练-free baseline。

Result: 实验显示，即使是较先进的 baseline 和当前 state-of-the-art 模型，在 LongEgoRefer 上也表现不佳，说明长时长第一视角场景中的稀疏目标定位与复杂交互理解仍是显著挑战。

Conclusion: LongEgoRefer 揭示了长时长第一视角时空 grounding 的内在困难，为 Video REC 研究提供了更贴近真实场景的评测平台，并表明未来需要更强的长视频理解、事件检索与细粒度目标定位模型。

Abstract: Egocentric videos capture rich and diverse human-object interactions and have emerged as a fundamental resource for understanding human activities related to objects. In this context, Video Referring Expression Comprehension (Video REC), the task of localizing the temporal and spatial extent of a referred object in video frames given a natural language query, plays a key role in linking textual descriptions to observed objects in untrimmed egocentric recordings. However, existing egocentric Video REC benchmarks primarily focus on short video clips, where some target object appears densely within frames. Such settings do not reflect real-world egocentric recordings, which are long-form, untrimmed, and characterized by sparse object occurrences and complex activity transitions. To address this limitation, we introduce LongEgoRefer, a novel and challenging benchmark constructed from long-form videos in the Ego4D dataset. LongEgoRefer contains 1,498 referring expressions with an average video duration of 45 minutes. The benchmark exhibits extreme target sparsity, detailed linguistic descriptions, and complex human-object interactions embedded in long, dynamic egocentric narratives. Consequently, it defines a demanding spatio-temporal grounding problem that requires models to identify both when an event occurs and where the referred object appears within extended video sequences. We evaluate existing Video REC approaches, including training-free baselines based on vision-language models combined with Grounded SAM2. Extensive experiments show that even advanced baselines and current state-of-the-art models struggle significantly on LongEgoRefer. These results highlight the intrinsic difficulty of long-form egocentric spatio-temporal grounding and emphasize the need for more robust video understanding models.

</details>


### [87] [WBMM: Windowed Batch Matrix Multiplication for Efficient Large Receptive Field Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02097)
*Wan Song,Wei Zhou,Rui Wang,Jun Yu,Toru Kurihara,Jiajia Xu,Shu Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WBMM（Windowed Batch Matrix Multiplication），用窗口化批量矩阵乘法替代大核深度卷积中的不规则 gather 访问，在保持/提升精度的同时显著加速训练与推理，并跨 GPU、CPU、边缘设备有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核深度卷积虽能扩大感受野并带来较强性能，但随着核尺寸增大，会因 gather 型计算导致不规则内存访问和吞吐下降；现有 LKA 在小特征图上有效，但在大特征图上可能变慢甚至不如未加速实现。因此需要一种更适合大窗口/大核场景、内存访问更规则且无需专用算子的通用替代方案。

Method: 提出 Windowed Batch Matrix Multiplication（WBMM）：将输入特征划分为连续窗口，通过索引紧凑的相对位置偏置表构造权重矩阵，并用批量矩阵乘法完成窗口内聚合，从而将不规则访问转化为规则矩阵计算。同时结合跨窗口通信机制与层级窗口重参数化，以缓解窗口隔离并提升多尺度建模能力。

Result: 算子级实验显示，14×14 窗口的 WBMM 在速度上优于 5×5 深度卷积基线，同时单层感受野扩大 7.8 倍。整网实验在 ImageNet-1K、COCO、ADE20K 上达到相当或更高精度，并带来 1.31–1.88 倍训练加速；在 GPU、CPU 和边缘设备上均表现出一致优势，且不依赖专用加速 kernel。

Conclusion: WBMM 通过窗口化连续内存访问和批量矩阵乘法，解决了大核深度卷积随核增大而效率退化的问题，具备“窗口越大吞吐越好”的特性，是一种兼顾大感受野、高效率和硬件通用性的视觉算子设计。

Abstract: Large kernel depthwise convolutions achieve strong performance but suffer from significant degradation as kernel size grows due to irregular memory access from gather-based computation; while Large Kernel Acceleration (LKA) helps on small feature maps, it becomes counterproductive on large feature maps, even slower than non-accelerated implementations. We propose Windowed Batch Matrix Multiplication (WBMM), which partitions input into contiguous windows and indexes a compact relative position bias table to construct weight matrices, enabling regular memory access via batched matrix multiplication. This yields a unique property: WBMM's throughput improves with larger windows, opposite to depthwise convolutions that degrade with larger kernels. Operator-level benchmarks show WBMM with 14x14 windows outperforms 5x5 depthwise convolution baselines in speed while providing a 7.8x larger per-layer receptive field. Combined with inter-block cross-window communication and hierarchical window reparameterization, WBMM achieves comparable or higher accuracy on ImageNet-1K, COCO, and ADE20K with 1.31-1.88x training speedup, and demonstrates consistent advantages across GPU, CPU, and edge devices without requiring specialized acceleration kernels. Our code is available at http://github.com/wansong-s/WBMM

</details>


### [88] [X-Splat: Gaussian Splatting for 3D CBCT Generation from Single Panoramic Radiograph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02099)
*Tomasz Szczepański,Szymon Płotka,Michal K. Grzeszczyk,Tomasz Trzciński,Arkadiusz Sitek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 X-Splat：一种基于高斯泼溅的框架，可从单张全景牙科 X 光片生成类似 CBCT 的三维牙科体数据，并在解剖结构重建上优于 NeRF/GAN 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张全景片生成三维牙科体数据可作为低辐射 CBCT 替代方案，但该问题高度欠定；现有隐式或生成式方法容易产生过度平滑几何或解剖不一致的幻觉，缺少几何驱动监督，难以精确定位锐利解剖边界。

Method: X-Splat 利用已知全景成像几何作为生成支架，将可学习的各向异性高斯基元沿形成输入图像的 X 射线路径初始化，并通过单次前向传播进行调整；训练中结合 Beer-Lambert 重投影约束、多视角放射影像监督和合成 PXR-CBCT 配对的直接体素监督；此外加入轻量残差细化器以注入数据集级解剖先验，并提出基于分割的几何感知评估指标。

Result: 在合成 PXR-CBCT 数据上，X-Splat 超越 NeRF 和 GAN 基线，能更好恢复单颗牙齿、皮质边界、牙槽结构以及以往方法难以重建的下颌管。

Conclusion: 将全景成像几何显式融入高斯泼溅表示可显著提升单张 PXR 到三维牙科体数据生成的解剖一致性和边界定位能力，为低辐射牙科三维重建提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Generating a 3D dental volume from a single panoramic radiograph (PXR) could provide a low-radiation alternative to Cone-Beam Computed Tomography (CBCT), but the problem is highly underdetermined: panoramic acquisition integrates 3D attenuation along curved X-ray paths into a 2D image, leaving depth-resolved anatomy unobserved. Existing implicit and generative approaches often produce oversmoothed geometry or anatomically inconsistent hallucinations, lacking geometry-driven supervision and relying on smooth representations unable to precisely localize sharp anatomical boundaries. We propose X-Splat, the first Gaussian Splatting framework for generating CBCT-like 3D dental volumes from a single PXR. X-Splat uses the known panoramic acquisition geometry as a generation scaffold: learnable anisotropic Gaussian primitives are initialized along the X-ray paths that formed the input image and adjusted in a single feed-forward pass, constrained by Beer-Lambert reprojection and multi-view radiographic training supervision. A lightweight residual refiner adds dataset-level anatomical priors without overriding the geometry already resolved by the Gaussians. We train on synthetic PXR-CBCT pairs, enabling direct volumetric supervision without paired real scans. We further introduce segmentation-based geometry-aware metrics, providing the first evaluation of PXR-based generation over maxillofacial anatomy. X-Splat outperforms NeRF- and GAN-based baselines, recovering individual teeth, cortical boundaries, and alveolar structure, including the mandibular canal which prior methods fail to reconstruct. Code will be available at https://github.com/tomek1911/X-Splat

</details>


### [89] [AbsoluteDegradation: A Physics-Inspired Synthetic Film-Degradation Pipeline and Archival Film Restoration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02131)
*Mikołaj Jastrzębski,Dawid Glinkowski,Dawid Zieliński,Daniel Borkowski,Wojciech Kozłowski,Kamil Adamczewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AbsoluteDegradation 合成退化管线与大规模真实档案影片基准，用于改进无配对数据条件下的档案影片修复训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 档案影片修复缺少成对训练数据，真实未损版本不可恢复；现有监督方法依赖合成退化但难以模拟真实、时序一致的胶片损伤。同时真实世界数据集在规模、质量和可访问性方面不足，导致评估不可靠、方法比较不公平。

Method: 提出物理启发的模块化退化合成管线 AbsoluteDegradation，将模拟到数字化过程建模为多类伪影的结构化组合，包括信号相关颗粒、参数化划痕、时序一致的相机运动等，从而可控地产生多样退化模式；同时构建包含 81,576 张高分辨率真实档案影片帧的评测数据集。

Result: 跨多种模型架构的实验表明，使用 AbsoluteDegradation 训练的模型对真实影片退化具有更好的泛化能力；新基准还能揭示当前方法存在的系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为档案影片修复提供了统一的训练与评测框架，有助于实现可复现且贴近真实领域分布的模型评估，并推动该方向的标准化发展。

Abstract: Restoring archival film remains a fundamentally challenging problem due to the absence of paired training data and the lack of standardized evaluation benchmarks. Pristine versions of deteriorated footage are physically unrecoverable, requiring supervised methods to rely on synthetic data that often fail to capture the complex, temporally coherent nature of real film degradation. At the same time, existing real-world datasets are limited in scale, quality, and accessibility, hindering reliable evaluation and fair comparison across methods. We address both limitations with AbsoluteDegradation, a physics-inspired, modular pipeline for synthesizing realistic film degradations, and a new large-scale archival benchmark. The proposed pipeline models the analog-to-digital process as a structured composition of artifact families, incorporating signal-dependent grain, parametric scratches, and temporally coherent camera motion, enabling controlled generation of diverse degradation regimes. In parallel, we introduce a curated dataset of 81,576 high-resolution frames sourced from real archival footage, designed for consistent evaluation under real-world conditions. Together, these contributions provide a unified framework for training and benchmarking restoration models. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures show that models trained with AbsoluteDegradation generalize better to real-world footage, while the proposed benchmark reveals systematic failure modes of current methods. We hope this work establishes a foundation for reproducible and domain-authentic evaluation in archival film restoration.

</details>


### [90] [AdaCount: Training-Free Similarity-Guided Spatial and Feature Adaptation for Zero-Shot Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02139)
*Muhammad Ibraheem Siddiqui,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaCount 是一种无需训练的零样本目标计数框架，通过相似性引导的空间重分配与特征调制增强 SAM3 在密集小目标场景中的计数能力，并在六个基准上达到训练免费 ZOC 方法的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 SAM/SAM3 的训练免费零样本计数方法可通过文本提示进行实例分割并完成计数，但在大量小目标、密集分布场景中，受限于图像分辨率与对目标相关区域关注不足，容易漏检和实例分离不佳，从而影响计数精度。

Method: 论文提出 AdaCount：首先基于原型驱动估计目标相似性图，用于定位与文本目标相关的区域；随后利用该相似性图进行两类适应：1）相似性引导的空间扭曲，将更多图像分辨率分配给目标实例区域；2）特征调制，增强编码器中特定目标相关表征。该方法保持全局上下文且无需模型重训练。

Result: 在六个多样化计数基准上的实验表明，AdaCount 在训练免费零样本目标计数方法中取得新的 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: AdaCount 通过显式利用目标相似性信息来优化空间与特征资源分配，有效缓解 SAM3 在密集小目标场景下的漏检和分割不充分问题，是一种高效且无需标注训练数据的零样本计数方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot object counting (ZOC) aims to count instances of arbitrary object categories specified only through textual prompts. Recent training-free approaches leverage foundation models such as SAM to reformulate counting as a prompt-driven segmentation task, eliminating the need for costly counting-specific training data with point-level annotations. More recently, SAM3 introduced promptable concept segmentation, enabling the zero-shot segmentation of all instances corresponding to a text-defined concept. However, SAM3 struggles in densely populated scenes containing numerous small objects, where limited image resolution and insufficient attention to target-relevant regions often lead to missed instances and poor instance separation, hindering accurate object counting. To address this limitation, we propose AdaCount, a training-free framework for ZOC based on similarity-guided spatial and feature adaptation. AdaCount first estimates a prototype-driven similarity map that identifies target-relevant regions. This similarity map subsequently guides two complementary adaptations: (i) similarity-guided spatial warping, which reallocates image resolution toward target instances, and (ii) feature modulation, which amplifies target-relevant encoder representations. Together, these adaptations enable SAM3 to devote greater representational capacity to target-relevant regions while preserving global image context, without requiring any model retraining. Extensive experiments across six diverse counting benchmarks establish AdaCount as a new SOTA among training-free ZOC approaches.

</details>


### [91] [SAMoR: Motion Modelling for Articulated Objects of Any Skeleton and Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02148)
*Yuhao Zhang,Gerard Pons-Moll,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMoR 提出一种面向任意骨架拓扑的跨拓扑运动表示，将不同角色骨架的关节运动压缩为共享的少量功能部件 token，用于重建、迁移、文本生成与局部编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动生成方法多依赖固定人形骨架；针对可变骨架的改造要么难以在不同 rig 间共享统一运动词表，要么通过全局池化损失细节。论文观察到跨物种的单个关节难以一一对应，但功能性关节组（如人臂、狼前肢、鸟翼）存在可共享的运动结构，且关节名称能提供部分语义线索。

Method: 方法 SAMoR 使用图 Transformer 编码器，输入每个关节的运动特征、运动学图结构和关节名称嵌入；通过交叉注意力池化将可变数量关节压缩为固定数量 K=8 的部件级 token，并结合残差向量量化形成跨 rig 共享的离散运动码本。为避免部件查询退化为冗余全局表示，引入拓扑无关的注意力监督损失，并使用关节名称 dropout 降低对文本标签的过度依赖。进一步使用 MaskGIT token 生成器支持文本条件生成与部件级编辑。

Result: 作者从 HumanML3D、Truebones Zoo 和 animated Objaverse-XL 构建异构语料，并在未见过骨架的角色上评估。SAMoR 在跨拓扑重建中达到 2.75×10^-2 的归一化 MPJPE，比最强的改造版 variable-J tokenizer 基线低 5.8 倍，同时在人形固定骨架 HumanML3D 设置上保持与专用方法相当的竞争力。

Conclusion: SAMoR 证明了以功能部件为中心、结合骨架图结构与关节名称语义的离散运动表示，可以有效统一不同拓扑的关节动画，为跨物种/跨 rig 的运动重建、迁移、生成和局部编辑提供通用框架。

Abstract: Modeling motion for articulated objects of arbitrary skeleton topology remains difficult: existing motion generators target a fixed human skeleton, and prior adaptations either fail to share a vocabulary across rigs or discard motion detail through global pooling. Our key observation is that while joint-level motion does not correspond cleanly across species, motion of functional joint groups does: a human arm, a wolf foreleg, and a bird wing share motion structure despite differing joint counts and connectivity, a correspondence that joint names (e.g., "forearm", "wing_L1") partially expose even when topology does not. We introduce SAMoR (Skeleton-Aware Motion Representation for Articulated Objects), a cross-topology motion representation that encodes each motion segment as a small fixed number ($K=8$) of part tokens shared across arbitrary skeletons. A graph-transformer encoder consumes per-joint motion features, kinematic graph structure, and joint-name embeddings, then compresses them into part-level tokens via cross-attention pooling and residual vector quantization, yielding a discrete motion codebook shared across rigs. To keep the part queries from collapsing into redundant global representations, we introduce a topology-agnostic attention supervision loss, with joint-name dropout to reduce over-reliance on text labels. We curate a heterogeneous corpus from HumanML3D, Truebones Zoo, and animated Objaverse-XL assets, and evaluate SAMoR on held-out characters with unseen skeletons. It supports accurate reconstruction and cross-topology transfer, and enables text-conditioned generation and part-wise editing via a MaskGIT token generator. SAMoR reaches $2.75 \times 10^{-2}$ normalized MPJPE on cross-topology reconstruction, $5.8\times$ below the strongest adapted variable-$J$ tokenizer baseline, while remaining competitive with fixed-skeleton specialists on HumanML3D.

</details>


### [92] [Patient-Specific Articulated Digital Twins from a Single Full-Body CT Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02156)
*Han Zhang,Boyang Zhao,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单次全身 CT 构建患者特异性可关节运动数字孪生体的概念验证方法，使静态 CT 模型能够模拟不同体位下的解剖结构与放射影像外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CT 衍生患者模型通常固定在扫描时体位，难以表示患者重新摆位后的解剖外观；而放射影像同时受成像几何与患者姿态影响，因此需要具备姿态可控能力的患者特异性解剖模型。

Method: 方法先将参数化人体模型 SMPL 拟合到单个全身 CT，以获得与患者对齐的运动学骨架；随后将分割出的骨骼和器官绑定到具备解剖感知的 rig 上，并在保持骨骼几何特征的同时进行目标姿态重定向；最后将重构模型渲染为不同姿态下的数字重建放射图像（DRR）。

Result: 在 3 名全身 CT 受试者上，拟合骨架达到 15.8±4.0 mm Chamfer 距离和 95.9±1.8% 骨骼包围率；采集姿态下重组模型的 DRR 保留主要放射结构，SSIM 为 0.872±0.016，PSNR 为 18.5±1.4 dB；在未见目标姿态下仍保持较高骨骼包围率 94.4±0.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从静态 CT 构建姿态可控的患者特异性解剖数字孪生体具有可行性，可为未来合成影像生成、患者摆位研究和影像引导相关算法开发提供基础。

Abstract: Patient-specific anatomical models provide individualized context for surgical planning, image-guided intervention, and algorithm development. However, most CT-derived models are static: they preserve the body configuration captured at scan time, but cannot represent how the same anatomy would appear after patient repositioning. This limitation is especially important for radiographic imaging, where appearance depends jointly on imaging geometry and patient pose. We present a proof-of-concept for constructing a patient-specific articulated digital twin from a single full-body CT scan. The method fits a parametric human body model (SMPL) to obtain a patient-aligned kinematic scaffold, binds segmented bones and organs to an anatomy-aware rig, and retargets body-pose changes while preserving skeletal geometry. On three full-body CT subjects, the fitted scaffold achieved 15.8 $\pm$ 4.0 mm chamfer distance and 95.9 $\pm$ 1.8% skeletal enclosure. Recomposition at the acquisition pose preserved major radiographic structure, with overall SSIM of 0.872 $\pm$ 0.016 and PSNR of 18.5 $\pm$ 1.4 dB across paired DRRs. Across unseen target poses, the resulting twins enabled articulation while maintaining high skeletal enclosure (94.4 $\pm$ 0.4%). As a feasibility demonstration, we render the articulated twin as pose-dependent DRRs. These results suggest the feasibility of extending static, view-controllable CT simulation toward pose-controllable anatomical twins for future synthetic imaging and positioning studies.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient PEFT Methods with Adaptive Checkpointing for Vision Models and VLMs on Resource Constrained Consumer-GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02158)
*Altay Toktassyn,Jurn-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种视觉模型在边缘设备显存约束下的参数高效微调与基础模型基线，发现QLoRA/BitFit在较小精度损失下显著节能，自适应梯度检查点可大幅降显存，而DINOv2在CIFAR-100上以更低能耗超过微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预训练视觉模型精度高，但微调时显存、能耗和训练成本较高，难以部署到显存预算有限的边缘设备。因此需要评估参数高效微调方法、梯度检查点策略以及不同基础模型范式在端侧约束下的实际权衡。

Method: 论文比较Full FT、LoRA、AdaLoRA、QLoRA、BitFit五种微调方法，覆盖ViT-Small、TinyViT、Vim-Small、MambaVision-T等Transformer与Mamba视觉骨干；同时评估无检查点、静态检查点和一种面向显存预算的自适应梯度检查点算法。基线包括OpenCLIP、SigLIP、DINOv2以及PaliGemma、MobileVLM、SmolVLM等模型，并在CIFAR-100和DTD上测量精度、训练时间、能耗和扩展后的NetScore指标。

Result: QLoRA和BitFit可降低约20–30%能耗，但带来约1–2%精度损失；自适应梯度检查点算法降低43–79%峰值显存，但增加9–30%能耗开销；DINOv2在CIFAR-100上以0.917精度超过最佳微调模型0.897，且能耗更低；小型自回归视觉语言模型在提示式分类中表现不具竞争力。

Conclusion: 在端侧显存受限场景中，QLoRA和BitFit适合追求能耗效率的微调，自适应梯度检查点适合严格显存预算下的训练，而自监督视觉骨干加轻量评估在某些任务上可能比端侧微调更具性价比。小型自回归视觉语言模型目前不适合作为高效图像分类方案。

Abstract: Modern pretrained vision models achieve strong accuracy but demand substantial GPU memory for fine-tuning, making edge deployment impractical. This paper compares five parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods (Full FT, LoRA, AdaLoRA, QLoRA, BitFit) on Transformers- (ViT-Small, TinyViT) and Mamba-based vision backbones (Vim-Small, MambaVision-T) under an on-device VRAM budget (e.g., 2 GB), together with three gradient-checkpointing strategies (none, static, and a proposed memory-budget-aware adaptive algorithm); and we evaluate three families of foundation-model baselines: zero-shot contrastive vision language models (OpenCLIP, SigLIP), self-supervised vision backbones with lightweight evaluation protocols (DINOv2), and autoregressive VLMs for prompt-based classification (PaliGemma, MobileVLM, SmolVLM). Experiments on CIFAR-100 and DTD report accuracy, training time, energy, and the NetScore family of multi-objective metrics, which we extend with two deployment-aware variants. QLoRA and BitFit cut energy 20-30% at a 1-2% accuracy cost; the adaptive algorithm reduces peak memory 43-79% with 9-30% energy overhead. DINOv2 surpasses fine-tuned models on CIFAR-100 (0.917 vs. 0.897) at a fraction of the energy, while small autoregressive VLMs remain uncompetitive.

</details>


### [94] [RadiomicNet: A Hybrid Radiomics-Guided Lightweight Architecture for Interpretable Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02185)
*Mohammad Amanour Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadiomicNet 将手工放射组学纹理特征与轻量级深度分割网络结合，在较少参数下提升医学图像分割性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习医学图像分割方法存在数学不可解释、参数量大、临床可解释性不足等问题；论文希望通过引入领域知识驱动的放射组学特征，在保持性能的同时降低计算开销并提供先验可解释性。

Method: 提出双流混合架构 RadiomicNet：以 MobileNetV2 为基础构建轻量级编码器-解码器，并设计 Radiomics Attention Gate（RAG），利用 GLCM 与 LBP 等手工纹理特征调制跳跃连接中的注意力；同时提出 Radiomics Consistency Loss，使纹理复杂度与预测不确定性保持一致。

Result: 在 BUSI 数据集上 DSC 为 0.763±0.231，在 Kvasir-SEG 上 DSC 为 0.854±0.112，分别较 U-KAN 提升 1.2% 与 1.8%，且具有统计显著性；参数量仅 3.27M，比标准 U-Net 少 9.5 倍，比 U-KAN 少 4.3 倍；ECE 从 0.142 降至 0.118。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，将放射组学领域知识显式嵌入轻量级深度网络，可在降低参数量和计算负担的同时实现有竞争力的分割性能，并提供更具临床意义的可解释性。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved remarkable performance in medical image segmentation, yet it suffers from critical limitations: mathematical intractability, substantial parameter requirements, and lack of clinical interpretability. We propose RadiomicNet, a novel two-stream hybrid architecture that enhances standard deep learning by integrating handcrafted radiomics features directly into the segmentation learning process. The key contribution is the Radiomics Attention Gate (RAG), which leverages Gray-Level Co-occurrence Matrix (GLCM) and Local Binary Pattern (LBP) features to modulate skip-connection attention in a lightweight MobileNetV2-based encoder-decoder, providing ante-hoc interpretability without post-hoc approximations. A novel Radiomics Consistency Loss further enforces alignment between texture complexity and prediction uncertainty, reducing Expected Calibration Error (ECE) from 0.142 to 0.118. RadiomicNet achieves a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 0.763 +/- 0.231 on the Breast Ultrasound Images (BUSI) dataset and 0.854 +/- 0.112 on Kvasir-SEG, outperforming U-KAN by 1.2% and 1.8%, respectively (p < 0.05, Wilcoxon signed-rank test), with only 3.27M parameters, 9.5x fewer than standard U-Net and 4.3x fewer than U-KAN. Gradient-based feature importance analysis reveals that GLCM dissimilarity (15.24%), GLCM energy (14.56%), and LBP entropy (11.49%) are the dominant radiomics cues, providing clinically meaningful explanations for segmentation decisions. The proposed approach demonstrates that compact, interpretable models grounded in domain knowledge can deliver state-of-the-art segmentation performance with substantially reduced computational overhead.

</details>


### [95] [MedSaab-US: A Backpropagation-Free Multi-Scale Wavelet-Saab Framework for Thyroid Nodule Segmentation in Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02209)
*Mohammad Amanour Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MedSaab-US：一种用于甲状腺超声结节分割的非反向传播、轻量级基线框架；其性能低于主流深度学习方法但参数少、CPU 可部署、数学可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习甲状腺结节超声分割方法通常依赖大量参数、GPU 训练和反向传播，部署成本高且数学可解释性有限，不利于资源受限场景应用。作者希望探索一种无需反向传播、轻量化且具有较强统计/解析基础的替代方案。

Method: MedSaab-US 结合多层离散小波变换提取多尺度空间-频率信息，并在 5×5、11×11、21×21 patch 尺度上使用通道级 Saab 变换提取特征；随后通过 Label-Assisted Greedy 特征选择保留判别性特征，并输入 XGBoost 进行像素级分类预测。Saab 参数由数据统计解析确定，XGBoost 通过贪婪树构建训练，不依赖反向传播。

Result: 在 TN3K 数据集上，使用 2879 张训练图像和 614 张测试图像，方法达到 Dice 0.4784±0.2190、precision 0.5768、recall 0.5604；模型规模低于 500K 参数，CPU 推理约 0.3 秒/图。消融实验评估了各组件、LAG 特征选择和训练集规模的影响。

Conclusion: MedSaab-US 可作为甲状腺超声分割中的探索性非深度学习基线，优势在于轻量、无需 GPU/反向传播、部署友好和较强可解释性；但 Dice 分数相对有限，说明其分割精度仍明显受限，尤其在等回声结节等困难病例上存在挑战。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) methods dominate thyroid nodule segmentation in ultrasound (US) images, achieving high Dice scores but at the cost of millions of parameters, GPU-dependent training via backpropagation, and limited mathematical tractability. These limitations impede deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we propose MedSaab-US, a backpropagation-free segmentation framework grounded in the Green Learning paradigm. MedSaab-US extracts multi-scale spatial-frequency features by combining multi-level Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) with multi-scale channel-wise Saab (Subspace Approximation with Adjusted Bias) transforms at patch sizes of 5 x 5, 11 x 11, and 21 x 21 pixels. Label-Assisted Greedy (LAG) feature selection retains the most discriminative features, which are fed to an XGBoost classifier for pixel-wise prediction. The Saab transform parameters are determined analytically from data statistics, while XGBoost employs iterative greedy tree construction without requiring backpropagation. Evaluated on the TN3K dataset (2,879 training and 614 test images), MedSaab-US achieves a mean Dice coefficient of 0.4784 +/- 0.2190, precision of 0.5768, and recall of 0.5604, with a model footprint under 500K parameters and CPU-only inference in approximately 0.3 seconds per image. We present this result as an exploratory non-DL baseline for thyroid ultrasound segmentation and analyze the specific challenges posed by isoechoic nodules. An ablation study further quantifies the contribution of each pipeline component, including separate evaluations of LAG feature selection and training-set size.

</details>


### [96] [DetailAnywhere: Fashion Detail Generation via Cross-Modal Feature Alignment Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02220)
*Zijun Li,Yimin Zhou,Jia Sun,Honglie Wang,Pengcheng Wei,Junlong Wu,Yongrui Heng,Jiyuan Wang,Huan Ouyang,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Dewen Fan,Qianqian Gan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出服装细节生成任务与FDBench基准，并用CFAD与一致性奖励优化提升聚焦区域细节图生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商生成式AI多关注虚拟试穿、海报生成和背景合成，但消费者在购买服装时还希望查看领口、袖口、面料纹理等指定细节区域；现有方法未显式建模从商品参考图中的聚焦标记到逼真局部细节图的生成问题。

Method: 作者形式化提出带focus conditioning的Fashion Detail Generation任务，构建包含4万余个人工验证参考-细节对、覆盖41类服装的FDBench；提出Cross-modal Feature Alignment Distillation（CFAD），利用微调的DINOv3教师模型，将多模态扩散Transformer的两个分支对齐到共享语义空间，并进行双分支蒸馏；同时引入一致性奖励模型，从三类质量维度联合评分图像对，并通过强化学习优化生成。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的DetailAnywhere在所有指标和人工评测中均显著优于现有开源SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 该工作定义并系统研究了服装指定区域细节生成这一新任务，提供首个大规模基准，并证明语义对齐蒸馏与一致性奖励优化能有效弥合参考图聚焦标记与真实局部细节视图之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative AI has achieved remarkable success in e-commerce applications such as virtual try-on, poster generation, and product background synthesis. However, when making online purchasing decisions for apparel, consumers also desire the freedom to examine specific detail regions of interest, such as collars, cuffs, and fabric textures, yet existing methods have not explicitly studied this setting. We therefore formalize a new, non-template task: Fashion Detail Generation with focus conditioning, and release FDBench, the first benchmark comprising 40K+ human-verified reference-detail pairs across 41 different categories. This task poses a unique semantic gap challenge: the model must bridge the correspondence between a focus marker on a product reference image and a photorealistic close-up view of the indicated region, while faithfully preserving the garment's identity, without any precise prompt. To bridge this gap, we propose Cross-modal Feature Alignment Distillation (CFAD), which leverages a fine-tuned DINOv3 teacher to align both branches of a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer in a shared semantic space via dual-branch distillation. To further improve consistency between generated details and reference images, we introduce a consistency reward model that jointly scores image pairs along three quality axes and optimizes generation via reinforcement learning. Experiments show that our model DetailAnywhere significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art opensource methods across all metrics and human evaluations.

</details>


### [97] [Efficient Waste Sorting for Circular Economy: A Confidence-guided comparison between One-Vs-All and One-Vs-Rest Classification Strategies with Human-in-the-Loop for Automated Waste Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02230)
*Mohammed Fahad Ali,Dominique Briechle,Marit Briechle-Mathiszig,Tobias Geger,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究面向德国市政垃圾分类场景的AI分类策略，比较OvA与OvR在总体分类和不确定样本识别中的表现，以减少误投并控制人工复核成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲各国及德国不同市政地区的垃圾处置规则复杂，居民难以正确分类 household waste，阻碍循环经济推进。因此，需要可根据具体市政分类方案配置的AI辅助工具，帮助居民正确投放垃圾。

Method: 研究基于德国Goslar市垃圾类别与分类规则构建数据集，评估两种多分类策略：One-vs-All（OvA）与One-vs-Rest（OvR）。除比较整体分类性能外，还通过设置不同置信度阈值，分析两种策略识别潜在误分类/不确定样本的能力，并将这些样本交由人工复核。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，但表明研究重点在于比较OvA与OvR在误分类控制和不确定样本筛选方面的差异，并评估不同置信度阈值下误分类数量与人工标注工作量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 可配置的AI垃圾分类系统有助于适配德国不同市政垃圾分类方案。通过置信度阈值筛选不确定样本并引入人工复核，可在降低误分类与控制人工成本之间取得平衡；OvA与OvR的选择应结合具体分类体系和复核资源进行评估。

Abstract: The complexity of waste disposal regulations across European countries poses significant challenges for the residents and hinders the transition to a Circular Economy. In Germany, the proper sorting and disposal of household waste remains challenging across municipalities. Consequently, substantially reducing incorrectly disposed waste is vital for improving waste management and advancing the Circular Economy. AI-based waste sorting solutions can support residents through user-friendly tools, such as mobile applications, that guide proper waste disposal. To be effective in supporting the Circular Economy, however, these solutions must be configurable to reflect the specific waste sorting scheme of individual municipalities in Germany. In the scope of this work, an evaluation and analysis are performed of two prominent classification strategies: OvA and OvR. The research uses a dataset constructed in alignment with the waste categories and sorting scheme of the city of Goslar in Germany. Moreover, this work aims to extend beyond the overall performance by examining the behavior of OvA and OvR classification strategies in identifying samples likely to be misclassified. These classification strategies are compared by applying varying confidence thresholds to identify uncertain samples for subsequent human review. This evaluation aims to balance the number of misclassifications against the human effort required for data annotation.

</details>


### [98] [When Token Compression Breaks: Structural Pruning vs. Token Reduction for Robust ViT Segmentation under High Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02237)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在统一 FLOPs 约束下系统比较 ViT 语义分割中的 token 压缩与结构化剪枝，发现 token 压缩在轻度压缩有效但高压缩下性能骤降，而结构化剪枝在高压缩下更稳定；进一步提出先剪枝再合并的组合流程，在高压缩部署场景取得更好的精度与鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 语义分割性能强但计算成本高。已有 token 压缩方法主要在低到中等压缩率下评估，缺乏对激进压缩和输入扰动场景的理解；结构化剪枝作为另一类正交的效率提升方式，也很少与 token 压缩在统一协议下公平比较。

Method: 作者在 ADE20K、Cityscapes 及其常见扰动版本 ADE20K-C、Cityscapes-C 上，对代表性的 token 压缩方法和结构化剪枝方法进行基准测试，并在匹配 FLOPs 的条件下比较干净输入与扰动输入上的语义分割表现；随后研究一种 prune-then-merge 流程，即先对 ViT 主干进行中等程度结构化剪枝，再叠加中等 token 压缩。

Result: 实验显示：token 压缩在轻度计算削减时效果很好，但在严重压缩时性能快速下降，说明过度减少 token 会导致显著信息损失；结构化剪枝的性能退化曲线更平滑，在高压缩场景下更稳定。组合策略在相近 FLOPs 下，在高压缩区间持续获得更优的准确率—鲁棒性权衡。

Conclusion: 对于部署导向的 ViT 语义分割，高压缩场景不应单纯依赖激进 token 压缩；中等结构化剪枝结合中等 token 合并/压缩更具实用价值，可在计算效率、分割精度和扰动鲁棒性之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are strong backbones for semantic segmentation, but their computational cost limits deployment. Recent token compression methods for efficient transformer-based segmentation reduce this cost by decreasing the number of tokens. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on low-to-moderate compression, leaving their behavior under aggressive compression and corrupted inputs unclear. Meanwhile, structural pruning provides an orthogonal route to efficiency by removing redundant components in the ViT architecture, but is rarely compared to token compression under a unified protocol. To bridge this gap, we benchmark representative token compression and structural pruning methods for ViT-based semantic segmentation under matched FLOPs on ADE20K and Cityscapes, together with their common-corruption variants ADE20K-C and Cityscapes-C. Our results reveal a consistent trend on both clean and corrupted inputs: token compression is highly effective at mild reductions but degrades sharply when compression becomes severe, consistent with substantial information loss from overly aggressive token reduction. In contrast, structural pruning exhibits a smoother degradation curve and is more stable at high compression. Motivated by these findings, we study a prune-then-merge pipeline that applies moderate token compression on top of a moderately pruned backbone. At comparable FLOPs, this combined strategy consistently achieves a better accuracy-robustness trade-off at high compression, offering a practical recipe for deployment-oriented ViT segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/phatnguyencs/vit-seg-compression.

</details>


### [99] [ArcAD: Anomaly-Rectified Calibration for Cold-Start Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02252)
*Ningning Han,Lei Fan,Jia Guo,Yunkang Cao,Xiu Su,Feng Cao,Donglin Di,Tonghua Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArcAD 是一个面向工业异常检测冷启动场景的即插即用校准框架，通过推拉式学习在少量正常样本和少量异常样本下构建更紧凑、判别性更强的正常边界，并在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实制造场景中的工业异常检测常面临冷启动问题：正常样本数量有限，无法充分覆盖正常分布；异常样本也很少，难以有效利用监督缺陷信号。现有方法因此难以形成紧凑的正常边界，并且异常判别能力不足。

Method: 提出 Anomaly-Rectified Cold-start AD（ArcAD），作为重建式工业异常检测基线的即插即用校准框架。方法采用 push-pull 学习范式：将有限正常样本投影到超球面并拉近为多个紧凑簇，以扩大对正常流形的覆盖；同时在超球面上合成伪异常，并结合真实异常样本将决策边界向内推，从而增强异常区分能力。

Result: 在 MVTec-AD、VisA、Real-IAD 和 MANTA 数据集上，ArcAD 在冷启动条件下的单类别和多类别设置中均显著优于现有有监督与无监督方法。

Conclusion: ArcAD 有效缓解了工业异常检测中的冷启动瓶颈，能够在数据稀缺条件下利用有限正常样本与少量异常样本学习更稳健的正常边界，提升异常检测性能。

Abstract: The deployment of Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) in real-world manufacturing frequently encounters a challenging cold-start bottleneck, in which limited normal samples fail to represent the full normal distribution and only a few anomalies are available. Under such a regime, existing methods struggle to form compact normal boundaries and fail to effectively exploit supervised signals from rare defects. To address this challenge, we propose Anomaly-Rectified Cold-start AD (ArcAD), a plug-and-play calibration framework for reconstruction-based IAD baselines. ArcAD follows a push-pull learning paradigm to construct a compact and discriminative normal boundary under data scarcity. On the one hand, ArcAD projects limited normal samples onto a hypersphere and pulls them into multiple compact clusters to maximize coverage of the normal manifold. On the other hand, it synthesizes pseudo-anomalies on the hypersphere and leverages real anomalies to push the boundary inward and sharpen anomaly discrimination. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD, VisA, Real-IAD, and MANTA demonstrate that ArcAD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and unsupervised methods in both single-class and multi-class settings under cold-start conditions. Code is available at: https://github.com/LGC-AD/ArcAD.

</details>


### [100] [AnyGroundBench: A Specialized-Domain Benchmark for Video Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02269)
*Rintaro Otsubo,Ryo Fujii,Reina Ishikawa,Taiki Kanaya,Kanta Sawafuji,Hiroki Kajita,Shigeki Sakai,Hideo Saito,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyGroundBench，用于评估视觉语言模型在专门领域的时空视频定位领域适应能力；实验显示现有模型在零样本与上下文学习适应上均表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STVG 评测主要依赖通用日常场景的零样本基准，难以反映真实专门领域中罕见视觉概念与复杂时空动态带来的适应需求；由于无法穷尽预训练所有数据分布，需要系统评估模型对新领域的适应能力。

Method: 构建面向领域适应的 AnyGroundBench，覆盖动物、工业、运动、手术、公共安全五类专门领域，整合新采集视频与已有数据集，并提供密集、高保真时空标注及专门训练子集；在实际计算约束下评测 15 个先进 VLM 的零样本泛化与上下文学习能力。

Result: 实验发现，当前先进 VLM 在专门领域的 STVG 任务中零样本泛化能力不足，且基于 ICL 的领域适应也未能有效解决问题，暴露出明显的时空推理缺陷。

Conclusion: AnyGroundBench 将 STVG 评估从静态零样本测试推进到领域适应评估；结果表明现有 VLM 距离真实专门场景应用仍有显著差距，未来需重点提升领域适应性与复杂时空推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated immense promise in Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG). However, current evaluation protocols are largely confined to zero-shot assessments on general, daily-life benchmarks. This creates a critical disconnect from real-world applications in specialized fields, where models inevitably encounter rare visual concepts and complex spatio-temporal dynamics. Since exhaustive pre-training across infinite data distributions is infeasible, the ability to adapt to novel domains is essential. To bridge this gap, we introduce AnyGroundBench, a domain-adaptation benchmark designed to shift the STVG evaluation paradigm from static zero-shot testing to rigorous domain adaptation. Targeting five specialized domains (animal, industry, sports, surgery, and public security), AnyGroundBench pairs newly captured videos such as expert-annotated mouse behaviors with established datasets, unifying them through dense, high-fidelity spatio-temporal annotations. Crucially, the benchmark provides dedicated training subsets to systematically measure domain adaptability. We extensively evaluate 15 state-of-the-art VLMs, assessing their zero-shot generalization and In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities under practical computational constraints. Ultimately, our findings reveal that current models fail in both zero-shot and ICL-based adaptation when confronted with specialized domains, exposing critical flaws in spatio-temporal reasoning that future research must address.

</details>


### [101] [AGVBench: A Reliability-Oriented Benchmark of Data Augmentation for Vein Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02271)
*Haiyang Li,Yuming Fu,Qun Song,Hongchao Liao,Jing Chen,Mounim A. EI-Yacoubi,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AGVBench，用于系统评估静脉识别中的数据增强策略，发现多图混合增强通常提升识别性能，但校准性和对抗鲁棒性较差，说明仅以准确率评价增强方法并不足够。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静脉识别面临标注数据有限和成像变化等问题；虽然数据增强可缓解数据不足，但面向自然图像设计的增强可能破坏静脉纹理与拓扑结构，从而影响身份判别。因此需要针对静脉识别场景进行系统、可复现、多维度的增强策略评估。

Method: 作者构建 AGVBench，在五个公开掌/指静脉数据集、七种骨干网络上评估 30 种代表性数据增强策略，网络覆盖经典 CNN、视觉 Transformer 和静脉识别专用模型，并从识别性能、校准性、对抗鲁棒性等维度进行比较。

Result: 多图像混合类方法，如 MixUp、PuzzleMix、StarMixup，整体上取得较强的识别性能；但这些方法往往校准较差且更易受对抗扰动影响。严重几何变换通常会降低识别效果，可能源于特征错位或空间裁剪；此外，增强策略在掌静脉与指静脉数据集上的效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 静脉识别中的数据增强不能只关注干净样本准确率，还应综合考虑校准、鲁棒性与安全性。AGVBench 提供了标准化评测协议，有助于推动可靠、安全、鲁棒的静脉识别系统设计。

Abstract: Vein recognition is a secure biometric technology often constrained by limited annotated data and imaging variations. While data augmentation mitigates this, strategies designed for natural images may disrupt the fine-grained topology and textures essential for identity discrimination. We present AGVBench, which evaluates 30 representative augmentation strategies on five public palm- and finger-vein datasets with seven backbone architectures, covering classic CNNs, vision transformers, and vein-specific recognition models. Our results show that multi-image mixing methods (e.g., MixUp, PuzzleMix, StarMixup) generally provide the strongest recognition performance. However, they are often poorly calibrated and vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, revealing a clear inconsistency between clean accuracy and adversarial security. We also find that severe geometric transformations frequently degrade recognition, which is potentially due to feature misalignment or spatial cropping, and that augmentation effectiveness varies across palm and finger vein datasets. These findings prove that accuracy-centric evaluation is insufficient for biometric augmentation. AGVBench provides standardized protocols to support reproducible research and guide the design of reliable, secure, and robust vein recognition systems. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/Advance-VeinTech-Innovators/AGVBench.

</details>


### [102] [FlowCIR: Semantic Transport via Flow Matching for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02284)
*Zhenqi He,Ziqi Jiang,Yuanpei Liu,Yanghao Wang,Teng Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowCIR将零样本组合图像检索建模为条件语义传输，在冻结VLM嵌入空间中学习轻量级传输场，并通过多负样本引导改善否定/移除类指令的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZS-CIR方法多依赖文本反演，将参考图像转换为伪文本token后与修改指令拼接，但这种文本空间组合容易损失细粒度视觉语义，且对复杂语义尤其是否定和移除操作不够稳健，同时训练成本较高。

Method: 提出FlowCIR范式，利用条件流匹配学习从指令表示到目标对齐查询嵌入的语义传输过程，并以参考图像嵌入作为条件。模型仅在预提取的视觉-语言模型嵌入上训练小型传输模块，不更新图像或文本编码器；此外提出推理阶段的Multi-Negative Steering，使含否定的相对指令远离被否定语义。

Result: 在标准CIR基准上，FlowCIR相较近期ZS-CIR方法取得强且有竞争力的性能；训练资源需求约为基于文本反演方法的十分之一，并在否定密集查询上提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将ZS-CIR视为冻结VLM嵌入空间中的条件语义传输是一种高效且有效的替代范式，可缓解文本反演拼接带来的语义损失与训练开销，并对否定/移除语义提供更稳健的推理机制。

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image by editing a reference image with a natural-language instruction, without relying on domain-specific annotated triplets. Most existing ZS-CIR methods rely on textual inversion to translate the reference image into pseudo-text tokens and then compose them with the instruction via simple concatenation in the text space, which can be lossy and brittle for fine-grained semantics. In this work, we propose a new paradigm, namely FlowCIR, that casts ZS-CIR as conditional semantic transport between reference and target embeddings. Leveraging \emph{conditional flow matching}, our model learns a lightweight transport field that maps the instruction representation toward a target-aligned query embedding conditioned on the reference image. Since FlowCIR operates on pre-extracted VLM embeddings and trains only a small transport module without updating the image or text encoder, it offers a computationally efficient training protocol compared with prior textual-inversion-based approaches. The resulting framework is training-efficient, requiring roughly $10\times$ fewer training resources than prior textual-inversion-based approaches. We further identify negation and removal as a major failure mode of VLM-based composition. To address this, we propose an inference-only Multi-Negative Steering strategy that steers a negation-containing relative instruction away from its negated semantics, mitigating the limited negation handling of VLMs and improving robustness on negation-heavy queries. Extensive experiments on standard CIR benchmarks demonstrate that FlowCIR achieves strong and competitive performance compared with recent ZS-CIR methods.

</details>


### [103] [DisciplineGen-1M: A Large-Scale Dataset for Multidisciplinary Visual Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02290)
*Zhaokai Wang,Mingxin Liu,Zirun Zhu,Ziqian Fan,Yiguo He,Mohan Zhang,Leyao Gu,Xiangyu Zhao,Ning Liao,Shaofeng Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Junchi Yan,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DisciplineGen-1M：一个百万规模、多学科、面向知识密集型图表生成与编辑的数据集，并基于该数据集训练了学科感知的推理生成模型，在多个学科与通用推理视觉基准上优于开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成与编辑模型擅长生成视觉上自然、美观的图像，但在知识密集型图表场景中可靠性不足。这类图像的正确性依赖学科概念、符号结构和精确空间关系，因此需要更大规模、结构化、可验证的学术视觉数据来支撑模型学习。

Method: 作者构建 DisciplineGen-1M 数据集，包含 120 万样本，覆盖数学、物理、化学、生物、地理、计算机科学、经济学、历史、音乐和体育等领域。数据构建采用可扩展框架，结合矢量图渲染、基于 OCR 的编辑、人工策划的程序化合成以及大规模文生图过滤，生成标题、编辑指令、结构化标注和具有可控语义差异的成对图像。在此基础上训练学科信息引导的推理生成模型，用于文生图与图像编辑。

Result: 在学科相关基准 GenExam 和 GRADE 上，模型相较开源基线取得显著提升；在通用推理视觉基准 WISE 和 RISE 上也表现出一定迁移能力。

Conclusion: 大规模结构化学术视觉数据是推动图像生成从“审美合理”走向“知识可验证”的关键要素。作者计划公开数据集、模型和数据构建流水线代码，以支持可复现研究和后续发展。

Abstract: Recent image generation and editing models can produce visually appealing natural images, yet they remain unreliable when the target image is a knowledge-intensive diagram whose correctness depends on disciplinary concepts, symbolic structure, and precise spatial relations.
